<script>
    $j(function () {
        $j("#tabs-menu ul.list-tabs li a").each(function (index) {
            if (index == 0) {
                $j(this).attr("href", "#" + $j(this).html());
            } else {
                $j(this).attr("href", 'https://www.bricoman.fr/brand/index/brandlistforMenu/' + 'isAjax/1/key/' + $j(this).html());
            }
        });
        $j("#tabs-menu div.brand-category").each(function () {
            $j(this).attr("id", $j(this).data('key'));
        });
        $j("#tabs-menu").tabs({
            beforeLoad: function( event, ui ) {
                if ( ui.tab.data("loaded") ) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    return;
                }
                $j('#imgLoadTab').show();
                ui.jqXHR.success(function() {
                    if ($j.trim(ui.tab.html()) != '') {
                        ui.tab.data("loaded", true);
                    }
                });
                ui.jqXHR.error(function() {
                    $j('#imgLoadTab').hide();
                    ui.panel.html("An error occured while loading brand infos");
                });
            },
            load: function(event, ui) {
                $j('#imgLoadTab').hide();
            }
        });
        $j("#tabs-menu div.ui-tabs-panel").each(function () {
            $j(this).addClass('brand-category');
        });
    });
</script> </div>

<script type="text/javascript">
        jQuery(document).ready(function() {
            var shopshome = '';
            var customer_groupshome = '';
            if(jQuery(dataLayer)[0].customer && (customer_groupshome == 'all' || jQuery.inArray(jQuery(dataLayer)[0].customer.type,['pro','premium siret'])) !== -1) {
                if (jQuery(dataLayer)[0].page && (shopshome == 'all' || jQuery.inArray(jQuery(dataLayer)[0].page.shop_id, ['all'])) !== -1) {
                    jQuery(".critizr-home").html("<a class=\"critizr-widget\" href=\"https://critizr.com/widgets/bricoman/"+parseInt(jQuery(dataLayer)[0].page.shop_id)+"/\" data-type=\"store_button:fact\">Critizr: Envoyer mon avis</a>");
                }
            }
        });

<!-- Modal -->
<div id="modal"></div>
<script type="text/javascript">
    var isShopPage = false;
    var currentShopId = -1;
    var bricomanReferer = true;


    (function($){
        $.Distance = function(place, options) {
            var base = this;
            base.options = {};

            base.geoloc = function ()
            {
                if(navigator.geolocation) {
                    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(base.getNearestFromPosition, base.options.fail);
                }
            };

            base.getNearestFromPosition = function (position)
            {
                var coord = position.coords.latitude+','+position.coords.longitude;
                base.getNearest(coord);
            };

            base.getNearest = function (origin)
            {
                if (base.options.url && base.options.success) {
                    $.ajax({
                        url: base.options.url,
                        data: { origin: origin, limit: base.options.limit}
                    }).done(function (ret) {
                        if (ret['status'] == '1' && $.isFunction(base.options.success)) {
                            base.options.success(ret['shops']);
                        } else {
                            var errorArea = jQuery('#store-locator-pop-in-alert-message');
                            jQuery('#chooser_modal').height(381);
                            errorArea.html(ret['error']);
                            errorArea.show();
                        }
                    });
                }
            };

            base.init = function()
            {
                base.options = $.extend({},$.Distance.defaultOptions, options);
                if ($.isFunction(base.options.onload)) {
                    base.options.onload();
                }
                if (place === null) {
                    base.geoloc();
                } else {
                    base.getNearest(place);
                }

            };

            base.init();
        };

        $.Distance.defaultOptions = {
            url: false,
            onload: false,
            success: false,
            limit: 5
        };


        $.Bricoman = {};
        $.Bricoman.Chooser = function (options) {
            var base = this;
            base.options = {};

            base.geoloc = function ()
            {
                var $button = $(this);
                var $text   = $button.html();
                $.Distance(null, {
                    url: base.options.geolocUrl,
                    limit: 3,
                    onload: function () {
                        $button.removeClass('modal_geoloc_grey_button');
                        $button.html('Recherche...');
                    },
                    success: function (shops) {
                        $button.html($text);
                        base.showShop(shops);
                    },
                    fail: function () {
                        $button.html('Géolocalisation impossible');
                        $button.addClass('modal_geoloc_grey_button');
                    }
                });
                return false;
            };

            base.showShop = function (shops)
            {
                $('#store-locator-pop-in-alert-message').hide();
                $('#chooser_modal_shoplist_list').html('');
                var $shopcard = $('.shopcard');
                $shopcard.hide();
                $.each(shops, function(key, shop) {
                    var $card = $($shopcard.clone());
                    $card.find('.shop_distance span').html(shop.distance);
                    $card.find('.shop_name span').html(shop.name);
                    $card.find('.shop_street').html(shop.street);
                    $card.find('.shop_city').html(shop.postcode+' '+shop.city);
                    $card.find('a').attr('href', base.options.chooser+'shop_id/'+shop.shop_id);
                    $card.appendTo('#chooser_modal_shoplist_list');
                    $card.show();
                });
                $('#modal-suggestion').hide();
                $('#modal-login').hide();
                $('#chooser_modal').height(500);
                $('#chooser_modal_shoplist').show();
                $('#chooser_modal_main').hide();
            };

            base.search = function () {
                var searchField = $('#chooser_modal_address_input');
                searchField.blur();
                $.Distance(searchField.val()+' France', {
                    url: base.options.geolocUrl,
                    limit: 3,
                    success: base.showShop
                });
                return false;
            };

            base.display = function (type, message) {
                $('#chooser_modal_login_alert').addClass(type).html(message);
            };

            base.displayLogin = function () {
                $.ajax({
                    method: 'get',
                    url: 'https://www.bricoman.fr/smile_retaileroffer/shop/reloadcustomerform/',
                    cache: false
                }).done(function(data) {
                    $('#modal-login').html(data);
                    $('#modal-login').show();
                    $('#modal-suggestion').hide();
                });
            };

            base.geoip = function(geoipData)
            {
                var params = typeof geoipData == "object" && geoipData.location ? {
                    origin : geoipData.location.latitude+','+geoipData.location.longitude,
                    limit : 1
                } : null;

                $.ajax({
                    data: params,
                    method: 'post',
                    url: base.options.bot_url
                }).done(function (ret) {
                    if (ret.autoGeoloc) {
                        jQuery('body').append('<form id="autogeoloc-form" method="post"><input type="hidden" name="auto_geoloc" value="1"/></form>');
                        jQuery('#autogeoloc-form').length ?
                            jQuery('#autogeoloc-form').submit()
                            : document.location.reload();
                    }
                    else if (ret.shopId !== 0) {
                        $('#modal').html(ret.modal);

                        $('#chooser_modal_address_input').autocomplete({
                            source: function (request, response) {
                                $.ajax({
                                    url: base.options.autocompleteUrl,
                                    data: { term: request['term'] }
                                }).done(function (ret) {
                                    response(ret);
                                });
                            },
                            minLength: 3,
                            select: function(event, ui)
                            {
                                this.value = ui.item.value;
                                jQuery('.shop-search-pop-in-form').trigger('submit');
                            }
                        });

                        $('#modal').show();

                        $('#chooser_modal_geoloc').click(base.geoloc);
                        jQuery('.shop-search-pop-in-form').submit(base.search);

                        $('#chooser_modal_connect').click(base.displayLogin);

                        $('#chooser_modal_shoplist_close').click(function () {
                            $('#chooser_modal_shoplist').hide();
                            $('#chooser_modal').height(350);
                            $('#chooser_modal_main').show();
                            $('#modal-suggestion').show();
                        });
                    }
                });
            }

            base.init = function ()
            {
                base.options = $.extend({}, $.Bricoman.Chooser.defaultOptions, options);
                var $shop = $.cookie(base.options.cookie);
                if (typeof $shop === "undefined") {
                    if (!base.options.is_shop_page) {
                        try {
                            geoip2 ? geoip2.city(base.geoip, base.geoip) : base.geoip();
                        } catch(e) {
                            console.log(e.message);
                            base.geoip();
                        }
                    } else {
                        var autoSetCookieUrl = base.options.chooser+'shop_id/'+currentShopId;
                        location.href = autoSetCookieUrl;
                    }
                } else {
                    if (base.options.is_shop_page && !bricomanReferer && $shop != currentShopId) {
                        var autoSetCookieUrl = base.options.chooser+'shop_id/'+currentShopId;
                        location.href = autoSetCookieUrl;
                        return false;
                    }
                    $.removeCookie(base.options.cookie, { path: '/', domain: 'www.bricoman.fr' });
                    $.cookie(base.options.cookie, $shop, { expires: 365, path: '/', domain: 'www.bricoman.fr'});
                }
            };

            base.init();
        };
        $.Bricoman.Chooser.defaultOptions = {};

    })(jQuery);


    /**
     * In case of non existance of shop cookie, launch shop chooser popin
     *
     */
    jQuery(document).ready(function() {
        jQuery.Bricoman.Chooser({
            cookie: "smile_retailershop_id",
            geolocUrl: 'https://www.bricoman.fr/smile_storelocator/shop/distance/',
            chooser: 'https://www.bricoman.fr/smile_retaileroffer/shop/chooserform/',
            bot_url: 'https://www.bricoman.fr/smile_storelocator/shop/saveShopForBot/',
            autocompleteUrl: 'https://www.bricoman.fr/smile_storelocator/shop/autocomplete/',
            is_shop_page: isShopPage
        });
    });
</script><!-- Popup Modal -->
<div id="popupModal">

<div aria-hidden="true" aria-labelledby="myModalLabel" class="modal fade" id="popup_modal" role="dialog" tabindex="-1">

<script type="text/javascript">
    jQuery(document).ready(function() {
        (function($){
            var $shop = $.cookie('smile_retailershop_id'),
                $popupName = 'smile_show_popup',
                $popup = $.cookie($popupName),
                $active = false,
                $popupContent = '',
                $key = '';

            if ($('#fragment_dynamic_popup_validity').length) {
                $(document).on("dynamiccontent:loaded:finished", function() {
                    if ($('#fragment_dynamic_popup_validity').find('#popup_validity').length) {
                        $active = ($('#fragment_dynamic_popup_validity').find('#popup_validity').attr('data-validity') == '1');
                        $key = $('#fragment_dynamic_popup_validity').find('#popup_validity').attr('data-key');
                        $popupContent = $('#fragment_dynamic_popup_validity').find('#popup_validity').html();
                        if (typeof $popup !== 'undefined' && $popup != $key) {
                            $.removeCookie($popupName, { path: '/', domain: 'www.bricoman.fr' });
                        }
                        if (!$.fancybox.isOpen && $active && typeof $shop !== 'undefined' && typeof $popup === 'undefined') {
                            $('#popupModal .modal-content').html($popupContent);

                            $('#popupModal').show();

                            $.cookie($popupName, $key, { expires: 365, path: '/', domain: 'www.bricoman.fr'});

                            $('#popupModal .modal-close .close').click(function () {
                                $('#popupModal').hide();
                            });
                        }
                    }
                });
            }

        })(jQuery);
    });
</script><div class="std"><div class="top-content">

<script type="text/javascript">
    var cookieMessage = $('allow-cookie-message');
    var isAllowedSaveCookie = /(^|;)\s*user_allowed_save_cookie=/.test(document.cookie);
    var isDisallowedSaveCookie = /(^|;)\s*user_disallowed_save_cookie=/.test(document.cookie);

    if(isAllowedSaveCookie || isDisallowedSaveCookie) {
        cookieMessage.hide();
    }else{
        cookieMessage.show();
    }

    if (isDisallowedSaveCookie) {
        jQuery('#modal-disallow-cookie-message').show();
    }

    function allowSaveCookie() {
        jQuery.removeCookie('user_disallowed_save_cookie', { path: '/', domain: 'www.bricoman.fr' });
        jQuery('#modal-disallow-cookie-message').hide();
        Mage.Cookies.set('user_allowed_save_cookie', '{"1":1}', new Date(new Date().getTime() + 31536000 * 1000));
        if (Mage.Cookies.get('user_allowed_save_cookie')) {
            cookieMessage.hide();
        }
    }

    function disallowSaveCookie() {
        Mage.Cookies.set('user_disallowed_save_cookie', '{"1":1}', new Date(new Date().getTime() + 31536000 * 1000));
        if (Mage.Cookies.get('user_disallowed_save_cookie')) {
            cookieMessage.hide();
            jQuery('#modal-disallow-cookie-message').show();
        }
    }

    function changeCookieSettings() {
        jQuery.removeCookie('user_disallowed_save_cookie', { path: '/', domain: 'www.bricoman.fr' });
        jQuery('#modal-disallow-cookie-message').hide();
        cookieMessage.show();
    }
